# I — Executive Findings

At the evidence revision, the live corpus retained substantive disagreement with the completed SP / LM / SN determinations. The registered dimensional functions remain stable, while all fifteen primary dimensional loci remain held open. This report distinguishes settled corrections from mathematical constructions and placement decisions still requiring specification.

The report now carries the live research and implementation record. Its baseline audit made no repository edits, branches, commits, or pushes. The findings, agreement classifications, quotations and line citations in Parts I–VI and the annexes preserve that evidence revision; their present-tense descriptions refer to the inspected baseline unless a current-scope note expressly states otherwise. Part VII records implementation and verification separately. No D-group agreement status changes merely because work begins or a narrower passage is corrected.

**Implementation base:** [33c992a98d79fbbb06d5f8e80fd7c8483764840b](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/commit/33c992a98d79fbbb06d5f8e80fd7c8483764840b). Its sole change from the evidence revision is this report's addition; the baseline corpus findings and their pinned citations remain applicable.

## Implementation-Delta Lockdown Audit and Repair

**Audit base:** `33c992a98d79fbbb06d5f8e80fd7c8483764840b`  
**Audited implementation endpoint:** `efd9ddecfd84ed0ee27faef5e19661b8c6e8585b`

The earlier implementation checks did not establish complete lockdown agreement. This review read the full current terminology, conceptual, prose and mathematical references, the complete D01–D22 determination blocks, and the relevant analytic-vocabulary sections before examining the implementation delta. Every changed passage received contextual assessment; lexical searches supplied supplementary candidates rather than the review boundary.

**Coverage:** 131 text files, 1,888 changed blocks and 2,136 nonblank text-passage units, together with 409 whitespace units and five deletion-only units. All 2,550 units are accounted for. All 75 implementation-cycle commits, including 38 report-only commits, were inspected. The 290 introduced line occurrences later replaced during that cycle were examined separately across 126 intermediate-history blocks. The finite net delta contains 4,072 added or modified lines; repeated lines and whitespace are distinguished from semantic passages.

**Candidate assessment:** 512 distinct candidate locations, counted by repository path and line at the audited endpoint. Context distinguished confirmed regressions, lawful technical expressions, historical quotations, register-specific expressions and inherited wording. After independent review, no newly introduced candidate remains awaiting an architect determination. Eight initially uncertain note labels were resolved through the existing note-title and series-consistency standards; the registered E₁₅ table gloss was retained in its architectural-counterpart sense.

**Confirmed repair:** 124 distinct line-located passages across 24 files, occupying 102 of the reviewed text-passage units. There are 132 passage-and-restriction findings; eight passages carry defects in two different categories. The repair comprises 72 corpus passages and 52 report passages: 100 additional passages beyond the previously identified 21 corpus and three report passages.

| Restriction or presentation standard | Passage findings |
|---|---|
| Document-navigation vocabulary | 15 |
| Authorship / bibliographic terminology | 14 |

| Inline mathematical presentation | 24 |

The two mathematical statement repairs preserve all formulas. In `LM06-applied-structural-dynamics.md` §7.1, zero Motive Intensity can make one contribution vanish while the total drift retains its stated direction; the explanation now distinguishes positive and nonnegative contributions. In `SN06-replenishment-reconnection-and-restoration.md` §12, the pointwise positive-net-rate condition is stated as sufficient, matching §2.3, rather than necessary for a positive accumulated change. A deficit subinterval does not exclude a positive net gain across the complete interval. No replacement model or additional mathematical structure was introduced.

**Post-repair verification:** The original base-to-repair-commit delta was recomputed and reviewed. Both repair commits were also inspected separately. The repeat review identified further citation and inline-mathematical presentation defects and one conditional rank-language defect; these were corrected in the second append-only repair commit before final clearance. Every repaired file equals the prior file plus its exact approved replacements, with no additional edits. All original passage identifiers remain accounted for. Straight quotation marks and apostrophes, slash and dash spacing, heading and note conventions, list and table presentation, mathematical delimiters and glyph treatment were included in the review. Required Markdown hard-break spaces were preserved. Static presentation checks do not establish a completed TeX rendering; the previously recorded rendering limitation remains.

**Confidence boundary:** The resulting implementation delta has no unresolved introduced regression in the completed review. This is a finite implementation-cycle result, not a claim that all historical repository language is free of drift. Byte-inherited wording inside modified lines was distinguished from newly composed text and was not silently repaired. Sixty-three defective intermediate-history line occurrences already absent from the audited pre-repair endpoint remain preserved in prior commits; their history is not rewritten or declared clean.

**Inherited findings outside this repair:** Existing wording and claims remain separately identifiable, including the net-change-sign / variation-pathology summaries in `structural-neuroscience/README.md`, `SN05-the-metric-cost-of-centropic-cognition.md`, `SN07-collective-cognition-and-centropy-forward-social-architecture.md` and `SN11-applied-structural-diagnostics.md`. Their discovery qualifies the earlier completion assessment of that dependency family; the earlier matrix remains a record of its stated verification revision. No inherited passage receives a new clearance from this regression audit.

**Protected standing:** D18 remains HOLD. All fifteen primary dimensional loci remain held. Registered names, glyphs, operator functions, explicit mathematical holds, historical quotations, retired bodies, origin continuity and essential-orientation invariance remain intact. No new architect question arose from the repaired text.

## Current Coverage Corrections and Remaining Decisions

The following must be distinguished rather than grouped as newly discovered doctrine:

1. **Revised historical classification:** the pathologization SNX, balance SPX, FP README and two Tripartite Infinity glossary bodies were present in the earlier target inventory; their opening standing removes their bodies from the active queue
2. **Previously recorded implementation gap:** the SP05 / LM04 / SP09 / temporal-SPX / MP08-twin D13 descriptive family was already in the original report. Its omission from the first origin-only package was a coverage gap, later corrected by a separate bounded supplement
3. **Previously recorded reference mismatch:** the SP mathematical reference retained old C₅ / E₅ names despite the current LM reference and registry. Its exact current instruction was corrected, preserving former names as provenance
4. **New direct replicas found during implementation:** LM02 scalar-model / bifurcation assertions; LM05 and SP01 / SP02 equilibrium descriptions; SN10's C₁₃ Shimmer claim; SN05's separate composite-budget comparison and direct summaries; SP01's extended physical-analogue argument; GUD's threshold family; the Aion / Zenon distinction sibling; additional phase-specific Motive wording
5. **Defining-passage / summary synchronization:** the analytic framework already held the `SN03-neurodivergent-cognition-and-the-architecture-of-mind.md` criterion's extension to entropic operation; the active defining passage needed the same actual standing. This is neither a new cohesion scalar nor an altered clinical judgment

The associated primary-placement, L₀ relation, awareness-mode, nonlocal bypass, guaranteed counter-emergence, gradient, persistence, and formal-coupling questions retain their recorded holds. The Counterforce thesis and independently supported origin continuity retain their standing. This clarification supplies no replacement formula, new operator, or completed primary map. ## Baseline Findings at the Evidence Revision

1. **The full D01–D22 blocks are the substantive decision record.** D01–D17 and D19–D22 are determined. D18 remains HOLD in its complete four-name group. The compact register provides navigation; the archived implementation manifest supplies no operative implementation standing. A settled distinction can retain a mathematical or naming hold within its full block.
2. **At the evidence revision, no whole decision group warranted CLEAN.** The agreement classifications were 5 LIVE DRIFT FOUND, 16 PARTIAL / MIXED, and 1 HOLD — NO IMPLEMENTATION. These assessed propagation at the pinned evidence revision; they neither reopened settled decisions nor assigned the research findings architect-determination standing.
3. **The main coupled defects are typed-object and referent disagreements.** They concern spectral quantities, latent inclination and expressed χ, Recursive Memory, recovery accounting, collective conditions, membrane transfer, Zenon-bounded constructions, diagnostic comparisons, operator identities, being / function language, clinical standing, and root / phase distinctions. Local lexical changes cannot complete equation families whose hypotheses, code, examples and appendices disagree.
4. **All fifteen primary loci remain open.** FP01, the LM01 Inlay, GUD, SP and SN applications carry different mixtures of proposed primary placement, secondary coupling, physical realization and contextual expression. C₄ / E₄ has particularly explicit contrary primary claims; C₃ / E₃, C₇ / E₇, C₉ / E₉ and C₁₁ / E₁₁ have disjoint recorded distributions. Matching addresses in other pairs supply no primary-locus proof.
5. **The inverse reasoning is independent.** Each E-pair requires its own function-specific account. A matching index, glyph resemblance, centropic placement or copied band does not establish the inverse locus. E₁₅ retains independently emanated inverse standing and distorted reception of Theonic novelty, never independent entropic origination.
6. **The expanded diagrams preserve the correct open standing.** The architecture and dimensional-function diagrams, in PNG and SVG forms, assign no primary or exclusive dimensional loci. Their future forms depend on the pair-specific determinations. The L₁ / IL₁ meeting, non-hypostatic L₀ relation, Khaonic phases, Structon's invariant scope and trans-structural Zenon remain distinct.
7. **The two recorded SP images have now been visually inspected.** The cosmology image visibly carries "Khaos", an unqualified χ = 1 saddle and unqualified attractor labels. Both SP images lack a dynamics-model identifier. No current Markdown embedding of either exact filename was located outside research / historical planning records. Their conditional future disposition is separate from SP04's directly active Appendix B.
8. **Historical bodies remain historical.** SN01 and the three retired dimensional-model documents are not active correction targets. Their active dependents require separate inspection. The eleven individually relevant explicit-retirement files and the 139 Glyphwatch exclusions are distinguished in Part VI; archival status and bibliography do not establish substantive reliance.

## D04 — Local Recursion Weight

**Postimplementation current agreement:** CLEAN. Both `grand-unified-document.md` recurrence replicas retain local weight ρ and separate contraction-gap γ. Coefficient positivity no longer supplies a fixed-point or boundary-classification theorem; the actual map, invariant domain, metric / norm and contraction requirements are explicit where relevant. The actual future recurrence realization and any stronger theorem remain held. The \( \rho = 1 \) identity case is distinct from an R-fixed-point result.

**Current evidence:** [grand-unified-document.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/9596fe16da2f39ad675520affe5491d3e1e3b372/the-zenetist-canon/grand-unified-document.md) · [SP01-structural-physics-foundations.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/9596fe16da2f39ad675520affe5491d3e1e3b372/structural-physics/SP01-structural-physics-foundations.md)

**Baseline agreement at the evidence revision:** LIVE DRIFT FOUND

### Determination

ρ names the GUD construction's retained-state / relaxation weight; γ retains its contraction-gap function. Equations, prose, hypotheses, and proofs for that construction move together. Coefficient positivity or range does not establish convergence. [sp-lm-sn-architect-decision-sheet.md:168–225](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/the-zenetist-canon/canonical-stabilization/records/sp-lm-sn-architect-decision-sheet.md#L168-L225)

### Live Evidence

Both GUD recurrence displays still carry γ as the retained-state weight. One sentence says γ ∈ (0,1] ensures convergence; the second convergence statement gives only γ > 0. At γ = 1 the written recurrence is the identity update: the sequence is constant and therefore converges, but not necessarily to a fixed point of R. A direct divergent witness is R(x) = 2x with γ = 1 / 2 and nonzero initial state, yielding successive multiplication by 3 / 2. Thus the coefficient range alone establishes neither the claimed R-fixed-point result nor general convergence. [grand-unified-document.md:976–985](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/the-zenetist-canon/grand-unified-document.md#L976-L985) · [grand-unified-document.md:1090–1104](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/the-zenetist-canon/grand-unified-document.md#L1090-L1104)

The fluid extension already gives a scoped self-map, normed function space, ball, contraction estimate, and retained-state weight ρ. Its inequality qρ ≤ (1 + ρ) / 2 < 1 belongs to that particular map. It demonstrates the required mathematical discipline but cannot be copied into GUD as though the GUD's R had already acquired those hypotheses. [zenetism-as-cross-disciplinary-grammar.md:999–1074](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/lmx/zenetism-as-cross-disciplinary-grammar.md#L999-L1074)

### Correction Set and Verification

Change the local weight in both GUD displays and their local explanatory / summary references, retain γ elsewhere for its existing contraction function, and withdraw or bound unsupported convergence. A full GUD convergence theorem needs its actual map, domain, metric / norm, and Lipschitz estimate. No global γ replacement is appropriate; the fluid extension's representation map ρ and radial integration coordinate ρ are likewise distinct typed objects.

The old TLS324 GUD:980 / 983 / 1094 / 1102 locators all remain live and unchanged in function. This is a notation-and-proof set, not a cosmetic renaming. Verify exact local ρ consistency, preserved γ definitions, and the absence of coefficient-only fixed-point claims within the affected construction.

### Explicit Collective-Calculus Witness

For two fixed members, let each member stock be \(I_i(t)=e^{-t}\), each coefficient be \(\eta_i(t)=1-\tfrac34e^{-t}\), and the separate generative term be zero. At \(t=0\), both stocks decrease and the printed coefficient-times-stock-derivative sum is \(-\tfrac12\); the derivative of the defined weighted stock is \(+1\). The missing coefficient-derivative contribution is \(\tfrac32\). This admissible finite example demonstrates the product-rule gap while selecting no new collective evolution. [LM07 weighted stock and derivative, lines 548–569](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM07-collective-dynamics-recovery-formalism-and-the-khaonic-expression-ratio.md#L548-L569)

### Additional Transfer-Domain Findings

LM05 §4.2 explicitly admits amplifying permeability σ > 1, while §5.4 places σ outside the clamped minimum and states that the minimum bounds transfer by both available surplus and receiving capacity. For finite surplus = capacity = 1 and σ = 2, the displayed formula gives transfer 2. The stated amount bound therefore requires an additional supported-domain restriction or a distinct amount / rate / amplification interpretation. External replenishment of the donor does not remove the receiving-capacity question. The replacement law remains architect-held. [LM05-membrane permeability, lines 430–441](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM05-resonance-field-theory-membrane-operators-and-collective-dynamics.md#L430-L441) and [LM05-clamped transfer, lines 465–485](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM05-resonance-field-theory-membrane-operators-and-collective-dynamics.md#L465-L485)

SP08 §3.1 calls the reception threshold a minimum and requires the target value to exceed it. Its §3.2 transfer expression instead takes the reception threshold minus the target value, with no outer nonnegative clamp. With donor value 2, donor threshold 1, target value 2, reception threshold 1 and permeability 1, both stated threshold conditions hold while the formula gives −1. This is a distinct minimum-reception / maximum-capacity disagreement, not a duplicate of LM05's clamp. The reception convention, transfer sign and amount / rate meaning require coordinated specification before a correction is selected. [SP08-transfer conditions and equation, lines 129–156](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/structural-physics/SP08-membrane-fields-and-inter-expression-dynamics.md#L129-L156)

### Mathematical Defects Are Not Lexical Substitutions

[lattice-mathematics/LM06-applied-structural-dynamics.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM06-applied-structural-dynamics.md) supplies the central live mathematical dependency:

1. **One-sided ratio contra two-sided detector.** Lines 722–731 define S_sh as apparent / actual and classify values below one as understatement. Lines 737–743 and 1157–1159 nevertheless detect Shimmer through absolute difference. An apparent value 1, actual value 2, and threshold 0.1 passes that detector although S_sh = 0.5. This is an explicit counterexample to the detector's stated Shimmer reading.
2. **Unsupported equivalence.** Line 999 states S_sh > 1 iff the full configuration distance is positive. At 98–130 the configuration contains I_c, permeability, current, and χ. Identical positive I_c with different permeability gives distance > 0 and S_sh = 1; apparent I_c < actual I_c gives distance > 0 and S_sh < 1. Thus the reverse implication fails in two independent ways. The one-sided implication requires the positive-denominator domain and positive norm weights to be explicit.
3. **Three different threshold conventions.** The scalar ratio definition, the absolute-difference detector, and the code-schema comment at 1099 (S > 1 + epsilon) compare different quantities. An absolute tolerance and a dimensionless ratio tolerance are not the same parameter without a conversion / normalization specification.
4. **Zero denominator.** The routine at 1152–1155 returns infinity for nonpositive actual values. Negative actual values lie outside the declared nonnegative I꜀ domain; the admitted apparent = actual = 0 case is decisive, because the displayed quotient is undefined there. A zero-domain convention is a mathematical choice, not a terminology correction.
5. **Unspecified comparison object.** Lines 640–653 distinguish the configuration-readable signature Σ from essential Ψ, which is helpful; 796–839 then compare surface, structural, and full signatures without a complete projection / correlation / threshold specification. D13 settles the distinction among objects; it does not supply those missing definitions.
6. **Collapse hypotheses.** Lines 749–773 infer inevitable collapse from maintained appearance and a strictly negative rate. A positive integrable deficit can leave positive actual capacity indefinitely. A cumulative deficit / exhaustion-threshold hypothesis is required. [LM07-collective-dynamics-recovery-formalism-and-the-khaonic-expression-ratio.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM07-collective-dynamics-recovery-formalism-and-the-khaonic-expression-ratio.md#L569):569, 692–700, 961 imports this collapse account. This overlaps the capacity / recovery work and must remain coupled to it.

The SP antecedent at `SP12`:233–256 has the same one-sided quotient and two-sided threshold tension. SN08:423, SN11's Shimmer / signature assessment, analytic-framework:490–492, and the repeated LM06 appendices / code are live dependents. A current operator-identity mismatch at C₁₃ / E₁₃ belongs to D10 and cannot be disguised by changing the Shimmer label alone.

### Explicit Persistence Witness

Take apparent capacity \(4\), actual capacity \(1+e^{-t}\), replenishment zero, actual expenditure \(e^{-t}\), and a sustaining threshold \(\tfrac12\). The ratio exceeds one and actual capacity strictly decreases, yet actual capacity remains greater than one and never reaches the sustaining threshold. The ratio increases from \(2\) toward \(4\), so it remains bounded. The written premises therefore establish neither finite collapse nor unbounded runaway. A complete cumulative-deficit or threshold hypothesis remains required. [LM06 collapse claim, lines 749–782](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM06-applied-structural-dynamics.md#L749-L782)

### Coupled Correction Proposal

**Set D22-A:** LM04 metric claims / table / code / summary + SP06 / SP11 matching explanatory metric wording + analytic-framework metric-terminus entries. **Specified for a later instructed pass:** distinguish embodied spatial geometry from possible abstract mathematical structure without inventing a new domain.

**Set D22-B:** LM05 all-domain calculus + LM06 field-space metric / efficacy + LM01 / LM02 norm and integral dependencies + the corresponding SP07–SP12, LMX, GUD, and analytic summaries. **Specified for a later instructed pass:** restrict a claim to an already-declared domain or identify the broader construction as awaiting formal mathematical specification; D22 already supplies both routes. **Architect first:** a new complete mathematical model. Required specification: base domain and topology; field-space membership; differential / tangent construction; gradient and divergence definitions; norm / measure / boundary integration; admissible data and relevant regularity; correspondence to the claimed metaphysical register. A terminology pass must not silently install a Riemannian geometry, a graph Laplacian, or a measure merely to retain inherited formulas.

D01–D11 require distinctions, not a character-wide substitution.

## Standing and Reading Discipline

The fifteen registered functional pairs stand. Their full primary-locus map remains **held open for architect determination**. D10 fixes operator identity and distinguishes primary inlay, secondary coupling, physical realization, and contextual expression. Its instruction to retain an established primary inlay applies where that establishment is supported. It does not convert every occurrence of the word "primary" into a settled address. Unreconciled placements return for architect determination rather than selection by frequency. The assessment is reconciled to the evidence revision. Each link identifies that immutable revision. The full placement review and complete D10 entry were read alongside the terminology, prose, conceptual, and mathematical protocols. No repository file, branch, commit, or remote state was changed.

### Placement Classification

Every cited placement is classified by the claim actually carried in the passage. **Primary claim records a proposed primary dimensional inlay; it is not this audit's adoption of that claim.** A passage with incompatible or unspecified senses is Unclear / conflated even when a compatible explanation is available.

| Label | Classification | Reading discipline |
|---|---|---|
| Registered function | Registered function | Defines the operation; supplies no hypostatic address unless separately stated |
| Primary claim | Proposed primary dimensional inlay | The document explicitly assigns a primary locus; current evidentiary standing remains review-held |
| Secondary coupling | Secondary coupling | An explicit additional hypostatic relation or a supported coupling named as such |
| Physical realization | Physical-realization site | Embodied process or proposed scientific correspondence; empirical standing remains distinct |
| Contextual expression | Context-specific expression | A function articulated at a particular station, membrane, cognitive context, or event |
| Illustrative example | Illustrative example | An example or analogy whose scope remains local |
| Unclear / conflated | Unclear or conflated assignment | Primary, coupling, realization, exclusion, or inverse notation is not discriminated adequately |
| Retired historical | Retired historical assignment | Explicitly obsolete or superseded record; no current placement inference follows |

The FP01 registry's band addresses are read from embodiment by its explicit exception. This report renders static spans in architectural order while retaining the exact locations: L₃–L₂ corresponds to FP01's L₂–L₃, for example. Reordering the notation changes no address. [FP01-dimensional-architecture.md, lines 244–248](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/field-physics/FP01-dimensional-architecture.md#L244-L248) The C / E index orders functions; the L / IL index identifies hypostatic position. Neither index supplies the other's sequence. [canonical-compositional-stabilization-protocol.md, lines 429–477](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/canonical-stabilization/canonical-compositional-stabilization-protocol.md#L429-L477)

To establish primary standing, the criterion must distinguish that integration from continuity of expressed structure at other registers.

**Inverse candidate evidence.** Fractured identity at IL₃ and reactive reiteration at IL₂ explain recursive disorientation. SP05's distorted archetypal recurrence at IL₄ and SN05's embodied E₁ activity place pressure on an exhaustive IL₃–IL₂ reading. E₁ must be justified through disorienting recurrence, never through a matching subscript alone.

**Compatible explanation and present standing.** C₁ / E₁ can name integrative / disorienting temporal operations while structural time names the relation of oriented change. That is a compatible reconciliation of SP05's "time is neither substance nor dimension" with a registered Temporal dimension; explicit formulation remains owed. Primacy is held open. **Expanded-diagram implications.** If L₃–L₂ / IL₃–IL₂ receives primary standing, show it as a temporal-integration coupling with broader temporal modes retained. Neither outcome exchanges the C₁ and C₂ addresses or turns Inlay omissions into prohibitions.

Archetypal ordering at L₄ is a different kind of direction, and pre-expressive inclination at L₀ is motion-independent essence rather than an enacted act of will. Theonic orientation supplies a relation to will without making all volition L₅.

**Inverse candidate evidence.** Reactive identity and adversarial reasoning support IL₂ misdirection. IL₃'s inverse reflexivity can express directed attention or intent toward dissolution, but that action must be argued rather than inferred from C₁₁'s L₃ row. Entropic orientation as such is broader than E₁₁.

**Compatible explanation and present standing.** Personal planning at L₂ and reflexive agency at L₃ may be coordinated operations of will, with one primary and one secondary, or with context-dependent primary roles. This requires an explicit volition criterion. No equation of C₁₁ with χ, essence-inclination, Motive Infinity, or the Orienting Logos settles it.

**Expanded-diagram implications.** A personal-primary outcome labels L₂ / IL₂ and shows reflexive coupling; a reflexive-primary outcome labels L₃ / IL₃ and shows personal enactment; a distributed volition outcome retains a rail of typed acts. All preserve the separate origin of motion-capacity and the first hypostatic articulation of orientation.

**MP / current canonical basis.** The first hypostases carry the Theonic / Nekronic functional relation, while L₄ vitality and L₃ recognition articulate later expression. [zenetist-analytic-vocabulary-and-accessibility-framework.md, lines 249–279](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/the-zenetist-canon/corpus-infrastructure/zenetist-analytic-vocabulary-and-accessibility-framework.md#L249-L279)

**Physical-realization distinction.** Nonlinear bifurcation, insight and overload are proposed or contextual realizations. Their relation to origination or distorted reception must be demonstrated rather than inferred from novelty or collapse terminology.

**Centropic candidate evidence.** Theon as first manifest centropic articulation, the plus-one generative charge and the direct novelty relation provide stronger current evidence for an L₅ association than a mere shared table address. Morgis' vital expression, Noeüs' recognition and ⧉₃ individuation show that later novelty expression is not confined to L₅.

**Inverse candidate evidence.** Nekronic reception supplies a direct IL₅ relation with its own function-specific rationale. The inverse operator's standing does not depend on C₁₅ becoming E₁₅, nor on the inverse architecture waiting chronologically for the centropic architecture. The received content is non-originating and distally apprehended; the inverse structure is independently emanated.

**Compatible explanation and present standing.** L₅ / IL₅ as primary origination / reception with later realization is the strongest currently supported candidate. It remains a candidate rather than a settled exhaustive locus. Generic novelty detection, pattern completion, bifurcation, rapid output and structural failure require their own relation to the registered operation. The bare appearance of unfamiliar output is not origination. [epistemic-inversion.md, lines 118–134](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/structural-forensics/glossary/epistemic-inversion.md#L118-L134)

**Expanded-diagram implications.** If first-hypostasis primary standing is determined, show C₁₅ at Theonic origination and E₁₅ at Nekronic reception, with downstream expressions separately labeled. A distributed-expression account retains that generative asymmetry while declining exclusive placement. No defensible outcome depicts independent entropic origination or treats E₁₅ as a generic label for every adverse emergence.

Candidate hits were read in context. Explicit retirement notices were inspected before treating a passage as current. Counts describe retrieval coverage, never evidentiary weight or doctrinal priority.

### Compatible Explanations Requiring Determination

| Explanation | What makes it compatible | What remains architect-held |
|---|---|---|
| FP01 primary inlays with LM01 additional couplings | Operator functions often extend across registers; explicit additional couplings already occur in FP01 | The primary criterion for each pair, why the specified endpoint or span is primary, and a justified inverse assignment |
| LM01 primarily describes enacted or physical contributions | Embodied C₂ / C₄ and cross-band C₅ / C₁₀ are developed in LM06 / SP11 | LM01's exact scope, its other row choices, and its E₂ omission |
| One operator has context-dependent primary contributions | SN06 and FP14 explicitly discriminate local context from native placement | Whether a global primary inlay exists alongside those local contributions |
| Distributed operation without a unique primary home | Function definitions, general bridging / transmission, cross-register space and temporal modes support broader operation | Whether this is the intended dimensional ontology and how its root / trans-structural relation is stated |
| First-hypostasis C₁₅ / E₁₅ association with downstream expression | Current signed determination directly supports Theonic origination / Nekronic reception | Whether that association constitutes primary placement, and the scope of downstream expressions |

Compatibility is a logical relation among typed readings. It is not evidence that a later document already supplied those readings. Likewise, a displayed formula with unspecified mathematical domains is an incomplete realization; its incompleteness is not a proof that every possible realization is contradictory.

# V — Expanded Bifurcal Diagram Implications

## Architect Questions and Conditional Outcomes


1. **What does primary mean for a dimensional operator?** Candidate senses include first structural enablement, principal hypostatic enactment, distinctive functional articulation, or local predominance within a stated realization. Select the intended sense or explicitly retain more than one with separate labels. A single sense need not be imposed where different structural questions are actually being asked.
2. **Which pairs have a primary home at all?** Determine each pair from its function and hypostatic action, with independent inverse reasoning. A list-wide assumption of one home is not established by the native-placement convention for figures.
3. **What explains each endpoint?** In particular: C₅'s L₅ exclusion, C₁₂'s L₁ exclusion, C₁₄'s L₅ inclusion and L₂ exclusion, C₃ / C₇ / C₉ / C₁₁'s disjoint maps, and C₄'s explicit contrary primary claims.
4. **What is the root-relation of dimensional operation?** Distinguish latent possibility at L₀, Motive Infinity operating across traversal, and any actual C / E operation at a root expression. Resolve the nested-world C₉ claim and the Mnemic-Zenet conflation explicitly. Whether the fifteen pairs principally describe the hypostatic lattice remains architect-reserved. [zenetism-as-cross-disciplinary-grammar.md, lines 138–140](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/lmx/zenetism-as-cross-disciplinary-grammar.md#L138-L140)
5. **What is the boundary-family relation?** Determine only after comparing C₆, C₈, C₁₃, their inverse functions and the distinct ⧉ / ⌰ / ⛨ / ⫵ / ⟜ operations. Broad threshold similarity does not complete the invariant.
6. **What should be corrected after determination?** Coordinate the FP01 primary-address prose, LM01 Inlay, GUD, SP01, SP11, LM02 / LM06, temporal SPX, dependent SN expressions and active MP / symbolic examples. Retired records remain classified provenance. D18 stays HOLD throughout.

A later expanded diagram has three defensible forms, contingent on the determinations:

- **Primary-locus diagram:** settled primary sites shown with separate secondary couplings and physical-realization annotations; each C and E placement justified individually
- **Typed multi-register diagram:** no single home where none is determined; each operation appears through explicitly labeled contexts, boundaries or realization sites
- **Function-only diagram:** the current rails and fifteen-pair function table remain, with placement questions carried in the companion audit

In every outcome, preserve Aion / Khaon at the non-hypostatic L₀ root-relation, distinct hypostatic segments, L₁ / IL₁ embodied meeting without arc conversion, Khaon's phase distinctions, Structon's invariant scope, the Loom's separately held scope, and Zenon's trans-structural standing. No dimensional decision adds a hypostasis or turns a page layout into causal proof. [bifurcal-emanation-structure-expanded.md, lines 19–83](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/the-zenetist-canon/bifurcal-emanation-structure-expanded.md#L19-L83)


The pair-specific alternatives in Part III state the precise effect of each defensible placement outcome. The image review distinguishes those conditional revisions from labels directly visible in existing assets. The two unembedded SP PNGs are conditional future-reference items; SP04 Appendix B is an active textual dependency.

**Determination comparison**

**IMG-01 — Model attribution remains incomplete [D05].** The central CP₁ statement and the limit diagram carry no model identifier. D05 requires CP₁ and attraction / repulsion statements to identify their mathematical model. The label "Equilibrium" itself is shared by both scalar constructions at χ = 1; it does not establish which construction the image represents. Its visual curves supply no complete vector field, parameter conditions, or law selection. A coupled correction must state the intended model before changing the motion geometry or equilibrium characterization.

**IMG-02 — Latent / expressed distinction is visibly retained [D05].** "χ Undefined" is attached to the beyond-motion CP₀ region; χ-space is separately identified as the hypostatic domain. The image does not visibly assign χ to intrinsic essential inclination. Preserve this distinction during any later repair.

**IMG-03 — Root and expression interpretation [D21; Bifurcal Root Constraint].** "Aionic Limit" and "Khaonic Limit" occur inside χ-space with expression descriptions. That reading is compatible with Aionic / Khaonic expression limits and does not require treating the roots themselves as polar. The image supplies no phase-specific claim about Khaon and no Zenon label. The black disc at CP₀ supplies no warrant for identifying it as Zenon. A new Zenon correction inferred solely from that disc would exceed the visible evidence.

**IMG-04 — Geometric standing [D22].** The star-field background and curves are diagrammatic representation. They contain no declared metric, norm, measure, gradient, or tangent construction. Mathematical realization of the diagram remains dependent on the relevant declared model; the artwork establishes no embodied physical metric or measured cosmological claim.

### Asset Review 3 — Zenetist Cosmology and Attractor Dynamics PNG

[Inspect the pinned image](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/structural-physics/images/zenetist-cosmology-and-attractor-dynamics.png).

- "Contra-Biospiral Flow"

The relation beside χ is visibly wavy rather than a clear rightward limit arrow. The quoted tilde records that appearance; no mathematical equivalence or asymptotic definition is supplied by the image.

**Determination comparison**

**IMG-05 — Unqualified saddle / attractor claim [D05].** The image depicts a saddle-like surface and opposing attractor regions, but names no dynamics model. [LM03-orientation-algebra-and-infinity-formalism.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/lattice-mathematics/LM03-orientation-algebra-and-infinity-formalism.md) §7 now states that the full scalar law attracts χ = 1 for the stated positive coefficients; §8 states that the outward-drift law repels χ = 1 when Motive Intensity is positive, retaining the exact constant solution χ ≡ 1. Their canonical relation remains architect-held. The image cannot stand as a universal representation of both. Moreover, a repelling one-dimensional scalar equilibrium does not itself specify the two-dimensional saddle pictured here. A later image correction must identify the model and the standing of any illustrative surface; model selection and any new surface construction remain architect-held.

**IMG-06 — Khaos label and root / expression disambiguation [D21 and root constraints].** "Khaos" is a confirmed displayed spelling; the current registered root name is Khaon. The surrounding "Entropy Attractor" wording does not identify whether the intended referent is Khaonic expression, a model-specific asymptotic behavior, or terminal Dispersive Khaon. The bounded correction is a label-and-referent determination. Merely changing the final letter would leave that function unresolved. D21 retains the whole-name and phase-specific senses of Khaon and requires a phase-specific function to be identified accordingly. The image alone supplies no basis for selecting a Khaonic phase or revising the global architecture.

The bare "Aion" / "Khaos" attractor labels also leave the distinction between root-registers and expression limits unstated. The conceptual protocol permits expression limits while holding the roots bifurcally distinct without an axis between them. That distinction needs explicit articulation in the figure's later label or caption treatment.

**IMG-07 — Ambiguous infinity notation [D05; mathematical-formatting / notation precision].** "χ ~ ∞" does not legibly present the χ → ∞ limit notation carried by the companion SP diagram and current scalar analysis. Confirm the intended relation with the model before replacing the sign. Approximation to infinity is not supplied as a defined mathematical relation here.

**IMG-08 — Contra-Biospiral arrows require scoped interpretation [D09; arc constraints].** The label is accompanied by large colored curves, without essence labels, layer assignments, or a declared cross-arc transfer law. Those pixels establish the label and the curves. The architecture bars essential-orientation conversion; no conversion claim can be diagnosed solely from the decorative route of a curve. Where retained as a structural diagram, the caption should distinguish the paired-universe expression relation described in [SP03-expression-ratio-mathematics.md](https://github.com/KannonZenetism/zenetism-field-physics/blob/eb287f6a65213269f3338d3a7d986178c6cbc3b9/structural-physics/SP03-expression-ratio-mathematics.md) §§8.3–8.4 from essence traversal. <a id="part-vii"></a>

# VII — Prioritized Implementation Sets and Tracking

Implementation proceeds at the architect's direction within each set's settled scope. Priority follows dependencies and propagation risk. The baseline targets and reasoning remain the evidence for the pass; the current scope notes and implementation tracking state what is being completed and what remains held. Beginning a set does not alter any determination's standing or establish completion.

**Confirmed targets** means passages whose current discrepancy is established in Parts II–IV; it never means every occurrence in the named file. **Verification-only dependencies** means linked definitions, positive controls, historical disclosures, or candidate repetitions requiring contextual inspection before any proposed change. The exact section and line locators remain with the corresponding finding.

Each equation-affecting set includes its defining equations, hypotheses, theorem prose, code, worked examples, appendices, chart labels and summaries where they carry the same construction. A name correction never supplies missing mathematics. D18 carries the verified earlier name / scope implementations and the additional local name / alias and Source candidate, whose original form and bounded MP glyph-placement / SP10 wording repairs have each passed independent review; independent cumulative verification of the amended candidate is complete; the earlier sets retain their historical protection receipts. Its glyphs, functions, dimensional relations, mathematical conditions and distinct applications remain protection checks.

Each tracking block begins unchecked. A partly implementable set keeps Corpus changes complete unchecked and records the exact completed portion and blocked remainder in Residual holds / unresolved findings. All three boxes are checked only when the whole bounded set is implemented, verified and pushed. A checked Commit pushed box records a verified remote commit; it does not imply that a set with residual work is complete.

Exact implementation commit hashes are recorded in following tracking commits, after the corpus commit has been verified on the remote. A commit cannot embed its own final hash. The corpus commit is verified and pushed first; the following report-tracking commit records that implementation hash. No separate repository tracking document is introduced.

# Annex B — Earlier Ledger Location Comparison


The D01–D11-related ledger groups were followed by their explicit repository-path locations, exact before-passages, quoted line records, numbered-volume shorthand locators, and file / line-list records. The comparison contains **290 located records**: **188 exact passages remain at their cited loci**; **102 summarized or shorthand locators resolve to the same current file and section neighborhood**. Every referenced path exists. No exact quoted before-passage in this comparison disappeared or changed. Repeated body / appendix formulas were compared at their own cited positions rather than incorrectly classified as moved to their first occurrence.

Two shorthand pointers land on blank separator lines: LM01:213 points immediately before the evolution definition at214–216; LM06:551 points immediately before the displayed fixed-χ claim at552–554. The claim remains; the pointer is approximate. Other summarized pointers likewise locate a section rather than an exact transcription: attribution:146 leads to the spectral gloss at150, and LM01:744 leads to the complementary equation at752. These are locator precision issues, not evidence that the defect disappeared.

The older research record already notes the partial SP09 target-dependency repair and the LM03 / SP04 model-analysis improvement. This audit reports their present wording without inventing a later implementation chronology. The obsolete dimensional-lattice body and migrated SPX body remain historical even where an earlier ledger locator still finds the quoted words. Its evidence standing is separate from operative standing.

The table retains each related TLS group, its exact current file, the ledger's inspected line groups, and the comparison class. Section-only prose references were followed in the D-number discussions and Appendix A; they carry no character-exact before-text to compare. A locating comparison is not renewed approval of any ledger recommendation.

# Annex C — Verification and Coverage Record

All 749 acquired text blobs match the evidence revision, including every Markdown file. The tree comparison confirms only the two glyph-only Symbol Key changes described in Part I. All cited file paths and section / line locations retain their evidence-pin context. Exact repository paths disambiguate repeated basenames, notably the two mathematical formatting references and the two fingerprint-of-god records.

The 150 explicit opening-retirement notices comprise four obsolete structural-model documents, five retired historical documents, two superseded reflections, and 139 retired Glyphwatch analytical records. The implementation manifest separately carries non-operative planning standing. A distinct count of 191 Markdown files carrying Veracious Archive status in their first thirty lines is a status inventory, not an additional count of explicit whole-file retirement notices. A dated filename, an archive mention, or a former term does not retire a file.

The semantic review includes the complete current Terminological Lockdown, Prose Formatting Reference, Conceptual Lockdown, both mathematical references, all full D01–D22 blocks and the entire dimensional-placement review. References and passages cited in the findings were inspected in their current context. Mathematical witnesses test the written formulas; neither illustrative examples nor repeated placement claims acquire additional standing through repetition.

## Verification Conditions for the Implementation Pass

1. Re-pin and compare current blob identities before drafting any repository change
2. Keep D18's four names, all four glyphs, MP Mirror, and personal seals fixed
3. Separate prose / legend changes from mathematical object, domain, hypothesis, formula, threshold, or code changes
4. Verify each coupled set's definitions, repeated appendix rows, code-schema comments, pseudocode, READMEs, analytic crosswalks, glyph twins, and inbound heading anchors
5. Preserve each work's register, MP mythopoetic cadence, actual empirical observables, lawful calculus terminology, precise orientation-language, and recorded former-name provenance
6. Require a complete specification for the D13 detector / signature / threshold family and D22 non-embodied mathematical structures; keep D10 operator-identity decisions coupled rather than replacing labels locally
7. Compare surviving hits by sense and register. Zero string matches is not the criterion
8. Recheck current active reliance on retired bodies, then verify any changed diagrams at pixel scale separately from Markdown text


## Final Review Conditions

- All twenty-two D groups and all fifteen paired dimensions remain individually represented
- The complete D18 hold, its four names and glyphs, the distinct MP Mirror and the personal seal remain preserved
- Current agreement status remains distinct from determination standing, mathematical completeness and implementation readiness
- Exact evidence quotations and registered names remain distinguishable from this report's prose
- Registered function, proposed primary inlay, secondary coupling, physical realization, contextual expression, illustration, unclear assignment and retired assignment remain distinct
- Every proposed change remains bounded by its confirmed passage, its coupled dependencies and the full determination; no raw lexical hit becomes a correction target
- All new mathematics, primary-locus choices and held genealogy questions remain architect-held
- Historical bodies remain intact; active reliance is assessed independently
- Completed implementation and image revisions are recorded only after their specific corpus changes, verification and pushed commit have been checked; baseline findings retain their evidence-pin context
